L-089 — M04R-09 seed and perfect-proposer ceiling audit

Question: Could an ANN or smaller deterministic seed materially reduce certified exact completion while retaining the same final neighbours and a complete stopping certificate?

Measured seed POCs: Seed 128 remains exact on IHRT current, UONEK current and AOUT historical. It evaluates 506 versus 512 rows on IHRT, but a looser UONEK threshold increases work from 4,181 to 4,781; AOUT remains 17,907. Seed 256 restores UONEK's final first threshold and 4,181 rows but saves nothing. These reject the assumption that fewer initial rows imply less certified work.

Complete ceiling: A fresh one-pass 24-query scan takes 1,653.26 seconds. For every query, the audit counts all candidates whose admissible composite lower bound is ≤ the frozen final τ. Exactly 70,037 of 71,745 current exact rows are mandatory; an ideal zero-cost proposer can remove at most 1,708/2.38%. An deliberately optimistic linear timing model projects only 55.28 seconds, or 1.69% of the complete 3,275.88-second batch. Thirteen cases have zero removable rows, including every heavy case. All gates and evidence digest 70e30fcd…33af pass without outcome access.

Resource-policy correction and negative block POC: CPU, memory and disk are now measured trade-offs rather than hard M04R-09 rejection caps. A 32,768-row UONEK scan remains exact at 4,181 rows, but scan time worsens from 102.15 to 131.70 seconds and peak RSS rises from 595.93 to 1,727.76 MiB. The obsolete 1,536-MiB POC gate labelled that run FAIL; schema v2 removes this policy-only failure, while the measured latency still rejects the variant.

Decision and next: ANN cannot materially improve certified exact completion or its tail under the present bound. Preserve it as a later low-priority/interactive comparison. The dominant opportunity is the 1,653-second shared bound pass. Next compare deterministic query-group/core parallelism on the 4-physical/8-logical-core host, requiring identical per-query proposal digests and complete certified results.

L-090 — M04R-09 query-parallel certified POCs PASS

Proposal topology bake-off: The 24 outcome-blind queries were partitioned across isolated spawned processes. Four processes × two Numba threads reproduce every serial proposal digest, eligibility count and unavoidable-row count in 622.83 seconds, a 2.6544×/62.33% improvement over the fresh 1,653.26-second serial control. Eight processes × one thread saturate all eight logical CPUs and improve this to 515.77 seconds, 3.2054×/68.81%, with digest a436cf1a…9c4b7. Eight × one is selected.

Complete-pipeline POC: A deterministic LPT scheduler uses only prior retrieval timing plus equal proposal cost to form eight groups of two to four queries. Each worker builds its causal queries, performs a local maximum-prefix scan, and completes exact certification sequentially with one exact worker. Proposal objects never cross processes. All 24 proposal digests, canonical matches, distance components, DTW paths and every certificate field exactly equal the frozen controls.

Performance: Complete wall time is 790.7009 seconds versus 3,275.8811, a 4.1430× speedup/75.86% reduction. Worker groups finish in 688.88–789.33 seconds. Maximum individual worker RSS is 874.39 MiB and summed worker peaks are 5,321.25 MiB. The increased resource use is accepted because correctness is unchanged and latency materially improves. Evidence digest a96791dc…c3909 rehashes exactly.

Boundary and next: This is a successful development POC, not yet the robust M04R-09 gate. Add atomic deterministic group checkpoints and resume validation, then an independent verifier that re-hashes the physical generation, baseline, every proposal/certificate/result digest, group accounting, performance arithmetic and outcome firewall. Preserve the 4× result only if those checks pass.

L-091 — M04R-09 resumable parallel gate and independent verifier PASS

Selected gate: The eight-process/one-thread design is frozen with 4,096-row blocks, 16,384/32,768 frontiers, seed 512, requested-position vector bounds and deferred final alignments. Deterministic LPT grouping uses only prior retrieval seconds plus equal proposal cost. Each group is bound to registry, generation, serial baseline, controls and exact membership.

Resumability and integrity: Eight atomic group checkpoints preserve every canonical match, component, DTW path and certificate. Schema v2 separates a reproducible retrieval-result digest from a checkpoint-integrity digest that also binds actual proposal/exact/elapsed/RSS values. A timing mutation fails integrity; a semantic mutation fails frozen-baseline comparison even after both local digests are recomputed. All four focused corruption tests pass.

Fresh and resume results: All 24 cases pass. Maximum group wall is 806.8174 seconds versus the serial certified batch's 3,275.8811, a 4.0602× speedup/75.37% reduction. Decision and next: M04R-09 is complete. L-092 — M04R-10 untouched 60-case NASDAQ contract PASS

L-093 — M04R-10 pre-authority store-coverage audit PASS

L-094 — M04R-11 truth-first runner and verifier ready

What: Implemented the write-isolated M04R-11 authority builder before starting the long run. It validates the sealed registry and full shadow generation, hashes its own committed runner, forms eight deterministic source-metadata-balanced groups, shares one bound scan inside each group, then performs exact certified completion and atomically writes each case.

Correctness gates: Every case requires 20 unique stable distance/ID-ordered matches, A/B tiers, maximum three per instrument, observed-session temporal eligibility, same-symbol overlap exclusion, exact-plus-pruned candidate accounting, strict stopping or literal exhaustion, ≤1e-12 quantized-bound excess, reconstructed certificate digest and unchanged query stock/benchmark prefix locks. Result digests omit measured timing; a separate integrity digest binds timing and RSS.

Resume and seal: Resume accepts only checkpoints bound to the exact registry, generation, contract, input prefixes, gates and both digest layers. The final matrix preserves registry order. SEALED.json is impossible until all 60 cases validate, and records that candidate results remained unopened.

Independent verifier: A separate implementation re-hashes the physical generation, reconstructs contract/source hashes, causal prefixes, engine input digests, temporal/diversity eligibility, every certificate and both checkpoint digest layers, then rebuilds matrix and seal digests. It will run only after the authority matrix completes.

Tests: Focused tests pass for checkpoint integrity mutation, ordering/accounting/overlap failures and deterministic complete balanced grouping. Full-suite execution is required before the runner is committed and launched.

L-095 — M04R-11 v1 frontier-cap failure and v2 recovery

Observed failure: The clean eight-process authority run failed closed after 44/60 valid checkpoints. nasdaq-GBNY-current-252 could not certify within the frozen 32,768-row operational frontier; no result was emitted for that case, no matrix or seal was written, candidate outputs remained unopened and forward outcomes were never accessed. The partial checkpoint-set digest is 850c0669…1389.

Diagnosis: All 44 completed cases certified at 16,384 rows. The first missing cases in the three interrupted groups are GBNY-current, ISPOW-current and FRMM-historical; thirteen later group members were not attempted. The cap was therefore a truth-construction liveness limit, not a distance, ranking or eligibility error.

Decision: Preserve v1 as FAILED_PARTIAL_UNSEALED. Its contract cannot legally accept changed execution code, so its 44 checkpoints will not be rebound or mixed. V2 keeps the frozen registry, generation, request, distance and causal prefixes unchanged, but explicitly supersedes the operational cap with deterministic doubling to strict certification or eligible-universe exhaustion. It continues to fail closed and never substitutes an approximate result.

Hardening: V2 binds the full local Python implementation manifest, requires the frozen eight-process setting, records typed overflow evidence for every frontier attempt, and separates semantic and measurement digests. A rejected intermediate optimization demonstrated that greedily discarding per-symbol scores is not incrementally safe when a later window overlaps multiple earlier selections. The corrected v6 execution retains every exact score as a compact match/instrument/start/cutoff record, discards no candidate, and reloads only the final 20 episodes for alignment. Regression tests preserve the counterexample. End-to-end timing includes every failed exact attempt plus the full shared proposal latency; amortized throughput is recorded separately. The independent verifier reconstructs case/matrix/measurement/provenance bindings, final distances/components/alignments and every primary/recovery proposal prefix in reverse scan order. A resulting seal means exact-authority correctness only; performance PASS/FAIL remains separately bound and cannot authorize production promotion. All 60 cases will be rebuilt in a separate root before verification and sealing.

L-096 — M04R-11 hard-tail measurement and v3 streamed closure

Why v2 was not launched: Real GBNY diagnostics showed that compact retention fixed the memory failure but geometric retries remained a poor execution plan. The 65,536-row run failed closed in 517.98 seconds at 560.07 MiB; 131,072 also failed closed in 834.11 seconds at 640.34 MiB, already exceeding the 600-second case gate. A one-pass bound census found 1,429,570 of 3,786,118 eligible rows at or below the unchanged 1.2923495 threshold. Blindly trying 262,144 therefore had no evidence of certification and would repeat prior exact work.

Measured bound decision: A deterministic 4,096-row hard-tail sample found zero admissibility violations. The exact native non-DTW partial sum retrospectively pruned 99.9190% at the observed threshold. Adding the existing symmetric LB_Keogh increased projected pruning by only 0.0631 percentage points, while costing 2,181.67 microseconds per candidate versus 566.35 microseconds for compiled exact DTW. The DTW bound is therefore rejected for this path; the already-computed native partial sum is retained.

V3 architecture: Keep the bounded 32,768 sorted prefix, classify it once, then stream every remaining eligible packed row in inclusive threshold bands without a global proposal tuple. Each admitted row receives the exact native partial sum. DTW is completed only when that bound is at or below the current constrained threshold; larger native bounds are retained as compact metadata. Because overlap deduplication can raise the kth threshold, a fixed-point closure reopens every newly exposed deferred row, and an incomplete selection drains all deferred rows. Certification requires both the minimum unclassified packed bound and minimum native-pruned bound to be strictly above the final threshold. Equality ties always complete.

Evidence hardening: The streamed scanner fails closed on non-finite/negative bounds, processes main and overflow rows in bounded blocks, binds the full packed-query input and exclusions, includes all boundary ties, and emits an order-independent admitted-set commitment. Forward/reverse scans with unequal blocks agree. The v7 certificate partitions the universe into exact DTW, native-bound pruned and packed-bound pruned rows. The independent verifier reverse-rescans every threshold band and reruns complete hard-case closure from raw OHLCV in reverse physical order before accepting a seal. Synthetic brute-force tests deliberately cap the prefix at three rows and reproduce identical ordered matches, totals, components and alignments.

Measured rejection: The real GBNY v7 fallback remained CPU-active after roughly 32 minutes and had already exceeded both the 300-second p95 and 600-second maximum case gates. It was stopped as a diagnostic without writing an authority artifact. The state machine remains a fail-closed safety fallback, but a 60-case v3 launch is not authorized on this execution path.

L-097 — Branch-aware denominator proof and v4 hard-case recovery

Root cause: The v1 quantized denominator always took max(IQR upper, std upper) because the exact distance can choose either branch. This is safe but needlessly weak when the outward quantization interval proves which branch distance-v1 must take. On 131,072 real GBNY-eligible rows, the old bound admitted 50,730 at threshold 1.2923495; the branch-aware bound admitted only 76. It was pointwise no weaker within numerical tolerance and classified 99.4100% of channel rows as certainly on the IQR branch.

Proof: If observed joined IQR is Î and candidate maximum quantization error is e, linear quantile Lipschitz continuity gives the outward interval [Î−2e, Î+2e]. When its lower endpoint is at least 1e−8, native distance-v1 must use IQR and only the IQR upper endpoint is needed. When its upper endpoint is below 1e−8, native distance-v1 must use the existing std upper bound. A straddling interval keeps the conservative maximum. Every branch retains the 1e−6 floor; equality at 1e−8 uses IQR. The original immutable 2,432-byte float16/error-radius row and distance-v1 are unchanged.

Falsification and real-data gates: Separately versioned contract distance-v1-full-f16-bound-v2, digest 5c1a8746…196c, passes 1,000,000 randomized/adversarial pairs and 15,625 float16 boundary pairs with zero scaled/unscaled violations. Across all 12 frozen NASDAQ authorities and 368,915 source-bound real rows, maximum component and total excess are zero, overflow is zero, and minimum pruning retention versus the native bound is 99.978769%. Evidence digests are e3491119…b5c0 and e6f55652…fed3; independent verifier v2 passes with digest 591764cc…578b.

GBNY final forward/reverse gate: After binding every v2 proposal to its complete packed-query input and every v8 certificate to all exact distance/alignment query features, independent forward and reverse full-universe runs are bit-identical in proposal digest, certificate digest, selected IDs, accounting and stopping values. Both scan 3,786,118 eligible rows, evaluate 1,114 DTWs, safely defer 69 by the native bound and prune 3,784,935 by the packed bound. The exact threshold is 1.2923495034522845, the next bound is 1.2923712364977704, no streamed closure is needed, and totals are 100.05/100.52 seconds at 711.25/710.95 MiB. Proposal/certificate digests are b18b02f4…dbfc/de49394a…e14.

Seal provenance closure: Authority v4 now refuses to start or resume unless the one-million-pair gate, 12-authority/368,915-real-row gate and independent verifier artifacts reproduce exactly. Their file and result digests, the branch proposal/threshold contracts, the complete implementation manifest, and the exact query representation digest are committed into the authority contract and independently revalidated before sealing. Focused tamper and stale-query tests pass, followed by the complete repository suite.

Independent traversal: A reverse physical scan with 8,192-row blocks reproduces forward proposal digest b18b02f4…dbfc, all 20 ordered match IDs and certificate digest de49394a…e14. After reserving the complete allowed numerical tolerance in every pruning decision, another concurrent forward/reverse reproduction remains bit-identical.

Versioning: V1 store/search evidence stays valid and byte-unchanged. The stronger scorer is proposal v2, certified search v8 and M04R-11 authority schema v4. The v7 streamed closure remains available only as an exact fallback.

L-098 — M04R-11 v4 authority correctness sealed and independently verified

Authority result: Fresh isolated construction completed all 60/60 frozen NASDAQ cases in 1,312.33 seconds wall time with zero invalid cases, worker failures, candidate-result access or forward-outcome access. Every case emitted 20 exact constrained analogues with complete accounting and strict tolerance-reserved stopping. Neither GBNY date nor any other case required streamed closure. Matrix digest is ae60b7cd…b29; correctness seal digest is 83501b4c…e2c9.

Independent result: The verifier rebuilt all 1,200 final match distances and alignments from source, rehashed the physical generation and proof prerequisites, rescanned all eight proposal groups in reverse physical order, reconstructed every case/certificate/matrix/seal digest and found zero failures. Verification digest is 99d11756…b190. Candidate results remained unopened and production promotion remains unauthorized.

Measured performance: Exact completion itself passes comfortably: p95 155.15 seconds, maximum 169.26 seconds and maximum worker RSS 680.46 MiB. The separately sealed end-to-end performance gate is correctly FAIL: the existing measurement assigns each case its group's complete 699–814 second shared proposal scan, yielding p95 931.48 seconds and maximum 957.40 seconds. This does not weaken the correctness seal and cannot be relabelled after seeing results. It identifies the next engineering task: either make grouped proposal latency meet the frozen per-query target or preregister a distinct throughput/amortized production contract before comparison or promotion.

Verifier performance fix: The first independent attempt exposed a sequential scheduler that would run eight reverse groups one after another. It was stopped read-only before producing evidence. The verifier now uses eight isolated one-thread workers, preserving independent reverse semantics while reducing wall time to the construction envelope; focused tests pass and a full repository suite is required before committing this verifier-only change.

L-099 — Preregistered scalar certified scheduler gate

Question: Is the failed 931-second p95 an engine limitation or a grouped measurement/scheduling artifact? The existing authority runner evaluates 7–8 queries in one worker scan and conservatively assigns that complete scan latency to every member. The independent single-query GBNY run completed proposal plus exact work in about 100 seconds, so this hypothesis must be tested across all 60 frozen cases rather than assumed.

Frozen experiment before execution: Run each sealed query as its own complete branch-aware proposal scan plus exact certified completion, scheduling at most eight independent one-thread processes concurrently. Each result must reproduce the corresponding sealed authority semantic digest and certificate exactly. Per-task wall time begins before source/query setup and ends after the case artifact is written, so cold setup/JIT cost is included. Checkpoints bind case, authority, contract and timing and are resumable; outputs are isolated under config/data/analogues/m04r12/scalar-certified-scheduler-p8.

Unchanged gates: 60/60 semantic equality, p95 end-to-end ≤300 seconds, maximum ≤600 seconds and peak worker RSS ≤1,536 MiB. No outcome or candidate-label data is used. A failure remains visible; authority measurements are not rewritten, and production promotion remains unauthorized regardless of this experiment until subsequent comparison/generalization/shadow gates pass.

L-100 — Scalar p8 result and preregistered p4 concurrency test

P8 result: All 60/60 independently scheduled queries reproduce their sealed authority semantic and certificate digests with zero failures. Maximum task wall time is 337.48 seconds and maximum worker RSS is 687.02 MiB, both passing. P95 is 307.61 seconds, failing the unchanged 300-second gate by 7.61 seconds. Result digest is edb6bc0f…5dbd. This failure remains preserved under config/data/analogues/m04r12/scalar-certified-scheduler-p8.

Diagnosis: P8 reduces p95 by roughly 624 seconds versus assigning every query its complete group scan, but the first-wave and largest exact cases show memory-bandwidth contention when eight full 3.82-million-row scans run simultaneously. Adding cores is therefore not the next test. Four-way concurrency should trade aggregate throughput for lower per-query scan and exact latency.

P4 preregistration before execution: Re-run the identical committed scalar scheduler, frozen 60-case registry, generation, authority digests and gates in a fresh scalar-certified-scheduler-p4 root with --processes 4. Task wall still includes source/query setup and cold JIT. Require 60/60 semantic equality, p95 ≤300 seconds, maximum ≤600 seconds and RSS ≤1,536 MiB. No result from p8 is reused as a timing checkpoint, and no threshold is changed.

P4 result: All 60/60 queries again reproduce the sealed semantic and certificate digests with zero failures. P95 is 216.90 seconds, maximum is 230.95 seconds and maximum worker RSS is 683.72 MiB; every preregistered gate passes. Total matrix wall is 2,134.20 seconds, intentionally slower in throughput than p8 in exchange for substantially lower per-query latency. Result digest is e87f67a4…91e4.

Independent verification: A separate verifier reconstructs every authority/scalar case digest, timing checkpoint, proposal/exact/task-wall relationship, ordered timing digest list, quantile, maxima and aggregate gate with zero failures. Verification digest is 304c4ab9…0c68. The measured deployment choice is therefore four concurrent one-thread queries for latency; p8 remains a preserved failed alternative rather than being silently overwritten.

L-101 — Preregistered untouched fast-candidate matrix

Question: Does the candidate-only system frozen in M04R-10 retain the newly sealed exact top-20 authorities across all 60 untouched NASDAQ cases, deterministically and within its original interactive limits?

It runs each query in a fresh isolated process, serially to avoid cross-query cache interference: explicit POSIX_FADV_DONTNEED, a forward 4,096-row cold scan, then immediate reverse 4,097-row and forward 4,093-row warm scans. Only after all 60 pools are sealed may a separate comparator open the authority root once.

Unchanged gates: every case retains at least 19/20 sealed neighbours; mean recall is at least 99% with 20/20 as the target; all three candidate digests and complete scan invariants repeat; row accounting matches the exact authority; temporal, same-symbol-overlap, tier and duplicate errors are zero; cold scan is at most 120 seconds, the second warm scan at most 60 seconds, and RSS at most 1,024 MiB. Passing this comparison still does not authorize production promotion.

Workers recheck both causal prefixes. Semantic and timing-integrity digests are separate, sealed roots are immutable, and whole-worker high-water RSS is gated.

L-103 — Preregistered lossless two-process legacy scan POC

Hypothesis: The rejected legacy scan is primarily CPU-bound while evaluating exact v1 lower-bound scores. This changes execution only: each row receives the same legacy kernel, and the final candidate/result digests must equal c8f51e20…2555/bb36c3b7…b75b.

Require exact three-scan identity, cold ≤120 seconds, second warm ≤60 seconds and the conservative sum of parent current RSS plus worker peak RSS ≤1,024 MiB. If two processes fail latency, a separately recorded four-process diagnostic may run; no untouched query is permitted.

L-104 — Preregistered bounded four-thread legacy scan POC

The producer now writes a seal only for a digest-valid 60/60 matrix with both aggregate gates true, validates every checkpoint when re-opening an existing seal, and leaves incomplete/failed runs unsealed. L-106 — Original M04R-11 combined gate failed on true cold storage

Observed result: The truth-blind v1 producer completed the first seven registry cases without opening any authority result. BLDP current/history, HAS current/history and FWONA current/history passed. GABC current preserved exact warm behavior at 30.57 seconds but its advised-cold scan took 267.57 seconds, exceeding the frozen 120-second limit. The run was interrupted after that terminal gate failure rather than consuming the remaining 53 performance cases. No matrix, success seal, comparison root or results-opened marker exists.

Diagnosis: The immutable pack is 8.6 GiB: 3,786,156 rows × 2,432 bytes. During GABC the host sustained only about 27–31 MiB/s with 32–47% I/O wait, which predicts the observed 268–300-second true-cold traversal. Warm scoring remains near 30 seconds. The earlier development cold passes reflected cache residency despite best-effort POSIX_FADV_DONTNEED; they did not prove a storage-independent cold SLA.

Claims boundary: M04R-11 v1 is permanently failed and must not be resumed or relabelled. The seven cases are exposed for performance redesign, but their predictions remain authority-label-blind because no authority or candidate/authority comparison result was opened. The other 53 remain untouched for performance. A one-time truth-blind finalizer must validate the frozen launch contract and exact seven checkpoint prefix, record the single failed gate in FAILED.json, make both the producer and comparator refuse the failed root, and preserve all original artifacts. Any replacement uses new roots and reports a 7/53 performance-role split while retaining all 60 for still-blind recall.

Failure seal: The finalizer reconstructed the exact frozen launch contract, source-bound seven-case registry prefix, checkpoint semantic/integrity digests, six passes, GABC's sole cold-latency failure and absence of every comparison-open marker. It published terminal failure digest 64df2ed8…c7843, then reproduced it on an idempotent second run. Direct producer-resume and comparator commands both refuse the failed root before generation traversal or authority access.

L-107 — Resident-generation recovery selected after measured alternatives

Alternatives tested: Exact field dependency analysis shows every non-padding field is required for arbitrary-query legacy-v1 output. Zstandard retained 84.6–87.8% of row bytes; existing certified compact bounds prune only about 13–23%, so sidecar-plus-refinement I/O equals or exceeds the original on this storage. The original 8.6-GiB true-disk scan cannot meet 120 seconds without at least 73.2 MiB/s before scoring; observed throughput is about 32.8 MiB/s. Compression, compact bounds and silent cache assumptions are rejected.

Development POC: A byte-identical immutable generation mirror under /dev/shm was fully SHA/manifest/provenance verified, then scanned by the unchanged four-thread legacy engine. CAKE returned the exact candidate/result digests c8f51e20…2555/bb36c3b7…b75b across all three traversals in 34.14/38.86/38.23 seconds at 781.63 MiB worker RSS; evidence digest is 19058807…fa94. This is a resident-ready result, not disk-cold. Estimated true restart staging on the observed disk is roughly 5–6 minutes and must be a separate readiness SLO.

Preregistered direction: Build a fresh v2 contract and roots; never mix or copy v1 checkpoints. Bind the actual resolved tmpfs mirror, filesystem class/device, source and mirror sizes/SHA, generation/provenance and an atomic startup-readiness seal. Regenerate all 60 homogeneous truth-blind pools. The first seven are descriptive performance-exposed cases and the remaining 53 are the confirmatory performance cohort; all 60 remain blind for recall. Semantic candidate sealing and performance status are separate so an infrastructure timing miss cannot again erase the still-valid recall experiment. Production promotion remains false pending recall comparison, independent verification, a fresh restart/performance cohort and later shadow/fault gates.

L-108 — V2 preregistration hardened before the untouched run

Adversarial correction: A launch audit stopped the first v2 draft before any of the 53 confirmatory cases ran. The draft confused the physical READY schema with the producer binding schema, generated a contract from the launch-time code and then validated it against itself, trusted worker-authored gates too broadly, did not terminalize every partial-write path and indirectly mixed startup timings into semantic evidence. The replacement requires a committed fixed-path preregistration, exact clean-Git implementation binding, a fresh empty root, create-only atomic case bundles, a hash-chained case_started/case_completed ledger, strict parent reconstruction, per-attempt file-identity leases, final full-content revalidation and terminal no-resume failure evidence. The comparator and its separate verifier are bound before truth can open.

Measured performance choice: The later user direction explicitly prioritizes correctness and latency over the old 1,024-MiB development ceiling. On the already-exposed CAKE case, eight outer threads preserved candidate/result digests c8f51e20…2555/bb36c3b7…b75b and completed the formal 4K-block traversals in 27.81/28.51/29.37 seconds at 1,210.72 MiB. Six and seven threads took 31.67 and 28.95 seconds; 1K, 2K, 8K and 16K blocks took 58.53, 34.04, 31.68 and 34.56 seconds. V2 therefore freezes eight threads, 4,096/4,097/4,093 rows and a measured 1,536-MiB ceiling. The old POC's sole 1,024-MiB failure is retained under digest 603435e9…1828; it is not relabelled.

Storage claim narrowed: tmpfs is not guaranteed unswappable RAM on this host because swap is enabled. READY v2 therefore claims only tmpfs-backed empirical query latency. It rejects symbolic-link escapes and active pointers, verifies contained regular files on the same tmpfs device, binds a timing/path/device-free content digest for semantics, and records capacity, timings and the physical READY instance separately for performance. The already-verified generation was renamed within the same tmpfs from its development path to the exact frozen v2 root and resealed without recopying. Two full validations returned identical content/READY/seal digests 27dfa03e…437e/554cd13f…afb6/6388b708…a21b; fresh live observations remain separately digested.

Offline-verification closure: A final independent-verifier audit found that copied READY and lease digests alone could not reproduce the historical READY-file hash after tmpfs loss. Before preregistration, the resident-binding schema was therefore advanced to v2. The producer now captures the exact READY bytes plus the full parsed payload, verifies their byte hash against the live file observation and publishes them inside the create-only semantic evidence. Both the comparator and the separately implemented verifier decode the snapshot and independently recompute the READY-file, READY, content, seal and lease digests, strict file maps, paths and cross-links from durable source data. Corrupting only the captured bytes while recomputing the outer binding digest is covered by a rejecting regression test.

One-shot policy: Execute the 53 previously unmeasured performance cases first and the seven exposed recovery cases last, while restoring registry order for every recall aggregate. Semantic sealing is independent of timing pass/fail, but comparison requires terminal performance evidence. Before authority access, write and fsync RESULTS_OPENED.json; then require at least 19 of each exact top 20 and at least 1,188 of 1,200 overall. Perfect 20/20 is descriptive only. No result may authorize production promotion.

L-109 — Resident v2 execution passed, candidate recall failed

Truth-blind execution: Preregistration digest f67d619b…0809 was generated from implementation checkpoint bce6ebe and committed as the sole file in launch HEAD 882436a. Integrated preflight passed with exact candidate/result digests c8f51e20…2555/bb36c3b7…b75b, 27.35/27.81/28.03-second scans and 1,208.78-MiB peak RSS. The one-shot producer then completed all 60 cases in 4,586.04 seconds: every semantic gate, all 53 confirmatory performance cases, all seven exposed regression cases and all 60 operational limits passed. Resident content remained 27dfa03e…437e; terminal run digest is 68824adb…05c. No authority result was opened before the semantic and performance terminals existed.

Preregistered recall result: After durable RESULTS_OPENED.json, the comparison retained 1,174 of 1,200 exact top-20 neighbours, below the 1,188 aggregate gate. Fifty-six of 60 cases were perfect. JCTC historical retained 16/20, GBNY current 9/20, GBNY historical 15/20 and ISPOW current 14/20, so the per-case 19/20 gate also failed. Comparison digest is cfda7ef0…b670. The comparison is terminal FAIL, the opened registry may never be retuned or called a holdout again, and production promotion remains false.

Failure diagnosis: All 26 misses are confined to those four previously untouched cases. Every failed query saturated the composite-1,000 and all seven component-64 heaps; every retained truth neighbour arrived through the composite route. The missing truth neighbours therefore fell outside composite top 1,000 and every component top 64. Evidence supports fixed-quota proposal truncation and loose bound ranking, not nondeterminism, eligibility, corruption or performance failure. The next development work must use exposed/development cases and a new untouched registry: measure composite 2K/4K/8K and component 256/512 ladders, multi-weight/Pareto unions, then prefer progressive widening plus exact rescoring and a lower-bound stopping certificate if it meets resource gates.

Verifier incident and repair boundary: The frozen independent verifier produced no result because its root-isolation rule rejected the project's exact Git-ignored artifact directory merely for being below the repository. Its next pre-output check also compared the real 83-file manifest (seven experiment entry points plus every src/**/*.py file) with only the seven fixed entry-point names, making the documented real manifest impossible even though every frozen blob was intact. A third pre-output defect validated the sealed authority implementation against the later worktree: the sealed 75-file tree is uniquely reconstructed from commit b86f899d4f4d95932591635c5f508398633fe078 before the authority matrix seal, every Git blob is checked against the sealed manifest, and the canonical independent authority verification PASS 99d11756ed7542714635eca3fb75a43faed93881121d1f22d8d87426f4d6b190 is bound explicitly. That preregistered verifier is not relabelled as passing. A separately named post-open verifier permits only the exact committed-Git-ignored config/data/analogues layout with zero tracked evidence files, rejects aliases and overlapping roots, pre-scans exact candidate/comparison/authority trees and required registry/source files before reads, derives the complete manifest set from the frozen Git tree, verifies every frozen implementation blob from launch HEAD, and narrowly supplies those already-verified historical views only while executing the otherwise unchanged frozen reconstruction. It publishes a distinct post-open schema, explicitly records that it was authored after truth opened and cannot authorize promotion.

Post-open evidence validation: From clean repair commit 99ac010, the create-only verifier validated all 83 frozen launch blobs, the unique 75-file historical authority tree, canonical authority evidence, all 60 candidate and authority cases, the results-opened marker, comparison rows and both terminal seals. It reproduced 1,174/1,200 retained, four failed cases, comparison FAIL and promotion false. Post-open verification digest is 381e60f8…74bd. This is a PASS for evidence integrity only; it does not convert either the preregistered verifier incident or the recall comparison into a pass.

Repository seal note: The post-run full suite exposed that two small synthetic structural tests applied the old 1,024-MiB limit to process-global ru_maxrss, which includes pytest collection and plugin memory. Their accuracy and latency gates passed, but the harness peaked at 1,050 MiB. Only those synthetic test ceilings were raised to 1,536 MiB to match the current no-memory-constraint policy; frozen production verifier specifications and recorded evidence were not changed.

L-110 — M04R-12 quota ladder rejects fixed-quota recovery

Why: M04R-11 v2 established that the fast candidate path is deterministic, eligible and performant, but loses 26 exact top-20 neighbours in four cases because its fixed proposal heaps truncate too early. Before changing the production design, M04R-12 measures which route and quota actually recover those misses. This is deliberately retrospective development evidence on the already-opened four failures; it cannot select a policy, validate generalization or authorize promotion.

How: Commit 6161f72 freezes a truth-blind two-process harness, preregistration digest 2002a4e6…5796 and implementation digest bd7ae808…666ff. The producer accepts no authority or comparison root. For each failed query it performs one forward 4,096-row and one reverse 4,097-row physical scan with eight threads, retaining lossless composite top 8,000 and top 512 for each of seven component routes. Seventeen smaller quota configurations are then derived without rescanning. Live READY/content bindings and exact generation-file leases bracket every scan; the original 1,000/64 candidate digest must reproduce before the producer seal. A separate process reconstructs the exact sealed producer tree, atomically writes and fsyncs RESULTS_OPENED.json, and only then loads authority truth. Resume validators reconstruct exact schemas, ranks, rows, digests and terminal trees. Before launch, two independent audits closed seven blocking findings; 49 focused/adjacent tests and the full 405-test repository suite passed.

Truth-blind producer result: All four forward/reverse pairs were exact and every frozen performance/resource gate passed. Forward/reverse seconds were 15.87/15.16 for JCTC historical, 27.96/27.41 for GBNY current, 19.48/19.53 for GBNY historical and 27.17/27.21 for ISPOW current. Maximum scan RSS was 1,260.02 MiB, below 1,536 MiB; each sidecar remained below 32 MiB. Producer seal digest is fd385555…e98a1. The seal records no truth input and no real forward outcome access.

Retrospective result: Baseline reconstruction exactly reproduced 1,174/1,200. Raising all seven component routes from 64 to 512 recovered only one neighbour in total; raising any single component recovered at most the same one. Composite widening was the only material signal: 2,000 retained 1,183/1,200, 4,000 retained 1,186/1,200 and 8,000 retained 1,190/1,200. However, composite 8,000 retained only 17/20 JCTC historical, 14/20 GBNY current, 20/20 GBNY historical and 19/20 ISPOW current. Adding component 512 to composite 8,000 changed none of those counts and expanded the largest union from 8,349 to 10,855 candidates. Therefore all 17 fixed configurations fail the per-case 19/20 gate; none is selected. Terminal diagnostic digest is f8f17a1f…4d4a, with 68 derived checkpoints, policy selection false, promotion false and forward-outcome access false.

Independent terminal audit: The committed comparator was rerun idempotently and reconstructed all four max scans, 68/68 derived checkpoints, every prerequisite/seal digest and the exact 77-file, 23,477,166-byte terminal tree. Whole-tree SHA-256 was identical before and after at 864fae8f…8419. Artifact time order is producer cases, truth-blind seal at 04:16:48 UTC, durable results-opened marker at 04:17:08, first truth-derived checkpoint at 04:17:08 and terminal aggregate at 04:17:22. No file changed, and the repository remained clean.

Decision: Do not spend another untouched registry on an arbitrary larger quota and do not treat 1,190/1,200 aggregate recall as sufficient. Reuse the existing exact certified v8 design: one branch-aware composite proposal, logical 1K→2K→4K→8K→16K widening without rescans, exact rescoring to a constrained fixed point, and certification only when both the first unclassified packed lower bound and every deferred native lower bound are strictly beyond the final threshold. If the 16K frontier cannot certify, use the already-correct inclusive threshold-band streaming fallback; any incomplete certificate returns no exact result. The next implementation gate is a branch-aware threaded scanner with scalar/forward/reverse parity, synthetic boundary and mutation tests, exposed-case differential evidence, and only then a newly frozen untouched registry.

L-111 — Certified progressive widening implementation sealed for exposed-case validation

Why: M04R-12 showed that larger fixed proposal quotas improve aggregate recall but still miss the required per-case result, while component-route unions add essentially no recovery. The selected development direction is therefore a correctness-certified search rather than another guessed quota: one branch-aware composite scan, logical 1K→2K→4K→8K→16K widening, exact constrained rescoring, native-deferred lower-bound closure and a streamed threshold-band fallback when 16K cannot certify. The same implementation remains OHLCV- and market-agnostic; M04R-13 is only a retrospective differential on the four already-opened NASDAQ failures and cannot authorize promotion.

Implementation and hardening: The packed proposal scan now has opt-in branch-aware threaded execution with scalar/forward/reverse semantic parity, and certified widening can reuse one precomputed max-frontier proposal without rescanning. M04R-13 runs each fixed query in a fresh serial child, uses eight proposal threads and eight exact workers, binds immutable source and resident identities, separates semantic from performance status, forbids resume, seals the truth-blind producer before authority access and requires an exact committed output root. The post-open comparator and a separately implemented verifier reconstruct proposals, certificates, matches, accounting, closure bands, query prefixes, Git/environment bindings, authority bytes and terminal trees. Adversarial review closed native-bound, repeated-frontier, closure admission, copied-root, split-read, timestamp, source-identity and rehashed-evidence failures.

Finite-evidence prerequisite: Constrained overlap selection is generically non-monotone and can temporarily reopen top-k, yielding an infinite internal threshold that strict JSON cannot persist. Before formal preregistration, a separate create-only truth-blind diagnostic must run the exact four paths from the clean implementation commit and prove that every observed round and closure threshold is finite and JSON-safe. It binds config bytes, stock/benchmark causal prefixes, packed/certified query digests, generation/provenance, resident leases, forward/reverse proposal equality, terminal certification and runtime Git blobs. The diagnostic and preregistration must then be introduced together in one unique commit; the producer and independent verifier both require those exact committed bytes.

Pre-run verification: Three independent audit passes report no remaining P0/P1 blocker. The 110 focused diagnostic/producer/comparator/verifier tests and 67 adjacent certified/packed/resident/M04R-12 tests pass, and the complete repository suite passes all 524 tests. No M04R-13 diagnostic, preregistration, formal producer result or authority comparison existed at this checkpoint.

First diagnostic stop: The first truth-blind diagnostic attempt stopped on case zero before publishing any artifact. The certified result was finite and terminal at the 1,000-row frontier with 20 selected, but dataclasses.asdict preserved the engine's tuple containers while the persisted validator correctly required JSON arrays. The producer was corrected to canonicalize the complete certificate and match payload through strict finite JSON before validation or publication; a regression now exercises real tuple-shaped dataclass evidence. This was an evidence-encoding defect, not a search or threshold failure, and no preregistration or authority path existed.

Second diagnostic stop: The next attempt passed certificate validation and stopped before publication because the draft match schema required timezone-aware cutoffs while the frozen NASDAQ source and sealed v4 authorities canonically use timezone-naive ISO session dates. The producer and independent verifier now accept only canonical ISO cutoffs that are either source-naive or explicitly UTC, then independently cross-bind their nanosecond value to immutable proposal/universe metadata. Non-UTC aware values remain forbidden. This correction preserves exact equality with the existing authorities; again no diagnostic, preregistration or truth access was created.

L-116 — M04R-14 adversarial oracle qualifies the certified engine

Why: Passing four exposed market cases did not prove the progressive certifier across its structural edge cases. T14-01 therefore uses only deterministic synthetic OHLCV and a scalar brute-force oracle to test the production packed proposal and certified-v8 paths before any 60-case or untouched run.

Coverage: The oracle forces exact eligible counts 0, 19, 20 and 25, including certified top-20 refusal below K and exact 20/20 success; main and overflow winners; sparse and missing volume; genuinely distinct seeded OHLCV; ties at K; overlap exclusion; per-instrument caps; native deferral; threshold closure; repeated frontiers; duplicate episode IDs; raw and constrained underfill; and source/store content plus inode replacement. Proposal checks span scalar/threaded × forward/reverse traversal, while certification spans forward/reverse × one/two exact workers. IDs, order, metadata and alignments must be identical. Distances and components use the explicit production-facing absolute tolerance 1e-6 with zero relative tolerance; the exact boundary is accepted and the next representable value above it is rejected.

Verifier hardening: Early drafts were rejected because their seals could be rehashed into internally consistent forgeries and because proposal-only cardinality checks did not exercise certification. The final independent validator deterministically regenerates every source, query, universe, proposal, scalar result, certified result, certificate and accounting state from frozen seeds. It rejects 14 coordinated attacks after every inner and outer digest is recomputed. Runtime evidence binds the complete tracked src/market_analogues/*.py set, oracle entry point, production contracts, Python/platform/package versions, thread environment and CPU affinity. A build at H0 remains valid after an unrelated descendant H1, while non-ancestor history and stored, current, worktree or indirect dependency drift fail closed.

Publication and test result: Canonical output is fixed, protected from source/evidence overlap, create-only and file/directory-fsynced. Real subprocess hard exit and fixed-timeout termination both leave no output or temporary file. Deterministic double builds are equal. Two independent audits report no remaining P0/P1 blocker; all 31 focused tests, 117 adjacent packed/certified/M04R-13 tests and the complete 573-test repository suite pass. No real oracle artifact existed at this checkpoint; production execution must occur from the subsequent clean implementation commit and remains development-only.

Next: Commit this implementation, run and independently validate the canonical oracle evidence, then begin the bounded T14-02 exact-worker and scheduler calibration. Production promotion remains false.

L-118 — T14-02 v1 calibration retained after verifier catches schema drift

L-122 — T14-03 v1 reaches 60/60 but fails closed at timing-free certificate validation

Observed result: Corrected H0 46bc93e580cfb24acb4ff629809efd16cd96516b and sole H1 4fe4a4b415104d2ef401d8650ea7cb08f274df74 passed committed launch and production construction. L-123 — T14-03 v2 is semantically complete but performance and verifier gates fail

Observed result: V2 H0 47661d7eceb8ab3458896309d14be24973296684 and sole H1 e8f5215d90840ef1fc5dccc02808a9d956b62cb7 produced an exact 307-file, 60/60 COMPLETE.json tree in 4,752.09 seconds. Semantic PASS includes all certificates finite, forward/reverse equality and no authority/outcome access. Exact p95 117.43 seconds and end-to-end p95 161.44 seconds pass; end-to-end max 244.92 seconds passes. Exact max 197.83 seconds fails the original 180-second ceiling. Proposal RSS appeared to exceed 1,536 MiB for cases 49–60, so aggregate performance is FAIL and truth opening is forbidden.

Measurement diagnosis: The proposal process was launched with preexec_fn, forcing fork from a parent that accumulates durable case evidence. Linux resource high-water accounting survived until exec, so both wait4 and RUSAGE_SELF included the parent's growing pre-exec footprint. A corrected spawn POC retained 160 MiB deliberately in the parent yet measured the child below 128 MiB. On the real hard case, corrected proposal RSS was 1,162.68 MiB, with 20.75/20.13-second forward/reverse scans and zero process swap; the 1,536-MiB limit therefore remains unchanged.

Latency diagnosis: The slow case has 3,786,118 eligible candidates, 9,976 exact evaluations and 4,318 materialization groups. A targeted corrected-spawn repeat took 210.30 seconds at 363.17 MiB, proving a real heavy tail rather than memory contention. V3 keeps exact p95 ≤120 seconds, end-to-end p95/max ≤180/300 seconds and total ≤7,200 seconds, but freezes exact max at 240 seconds using exposed development evidence before any authority or untouched run.

Verifier finding and correction: The independent verifier failed before case replay because it expected a nonexistent schema_version beside the runtime state/digest. Producer and machine contract consistently define the two-key envelope. V3 corrects that expectation and adds a direct production-envelope regression. The runtime now uses taskset without preexec_fn, retains inherited wait4 RSS only as context, and gates on supervised post-exec /proc high-water evidence.

L-136 — Evidence-card semantics frozen before query-level aggregation

L-137 — Evidence-card production and independent synthetic aggregation agree

L-139 — T14-10 walk-forward falsification contract designed before historical queries

Why a new registry: The sealed 3,270 cards all query the 30 March 2026 snapshot. Their analogue outcomes are valid descriptive evidence, but the locked archive cannot reveal what happened after those present-cutoff queries. Calibration therefore requires separately frozen historical query snapshots whose realized outcomes are not opened until their predictions and baselines exist.

Outcome-blind population: The new contract selects up to 24 queries at each monthly benchmark close: four from each causal prefix-quality A/B × trailing-252-session liquidity low/mid/high cell, ordered by a digest-bound SHA-256 rule. The 2012–13 block seeds expanding baselines; 2014–17 is development diagnostic, 2018–23 contains three locked validation blocks, and January 2024 through August 2025 is the single-open final block. The initially considered September endpoint was rejected during pre-seal calendar verification because only 124—not 126—locked benchmark sessions remained before 30 March 2026; August retains the complete horizon.

Falsification design: The primary target is the frozen 20-session favorable/adverse/no-touch barrier route with ambiguity and censoring excluded but reported. Half-count Dirichlet/Beta smoothing prevents artificial zero/one probabilities. The primary forced-score lane includes abstained queries so novelty or instability cannot hide failures; a selective lane separately reports coverage. Composite weighted evidence must beat unconditional, regime, deterministic-random, recent-return-matched and price-only baselines on final Brier score, with locked log-loss, interval, fold and abstention requirements.

Dependence and selection control: Inference uses paired calendar-month losses, three-month blocks, 10,000 fixed-seed resamples, HAC/Diebold–Mariano comparisons and Holm correction. Hit rate cannot pass. Any threshold or representation change requires a new version and new final period. The plan adopts sequential/non-stationary interval guidance from Zaffran et al. and Retzlaff et al. and preserves the backtest-selection boundary from Carr, Bailey and López de Prado.

Hard product limit: A research calibration pass cannot become a production pass on this archive. Point-in-time membership, delisting returns, sector history and event-cluster identities are absent; the sector baseline is marked unavailable without substitution and poor_data_quality remains. The contract digest is 2c65288b…6a8e; thirteen focused mutation tests and a real dry validator pass with historical walk-forward outcomes and the final period unopened.

Next: Commit the contract, plan, validator and tests. Publish the create-only pre-outcome verification receipt, then build and independently verify WF-01's exact historical query registry before any retrieval or query future is accessed.

L-149 — Full-pack counts reject exact price threshold streaming

This decisively rejects an exact-streaming implementation for the current safe price bound.

Two-sided band-constrained interval minima divided by the maximum DTW path length safely lower-bound the exact normalized DTW. L-154 — Immutable aligned DTW sample-store substrate

Layout: Each 788-byte row stores four 64-point temporal float16 channels, four stable uint8 sorting permutations, four outward float32 radii, presence bits and alignment padding. At the frozen 3,786,156-row inventory this projects to about 2.78 GiB. Retaining order permutations avoids sorting during every query while using materially less space than a second sorted float16 copy.

Identity: Separate main and overflow arrays align one-for-one with the existing packed generation and bind its generation ID, main/overflow content hashes, counts and provenance. The auxiliary generation is itself content-addressed and immutable. File size, SHA-256, manifest self-digest, dtype, finite radii, presence zeros, true order permutations and actual sorted-center order all fail closed.

Focused verification: Synthetic main/overflow round-trip reproduces the scalar interval bound within 1e-12; absent fallback rows return zero. Duplicate order entries and post-publication byte corruption are rejected. No outcome or label is present in the store contract.

Next: Build the preregistered bounded real shard POC, including independently rebuilt parity and physical packed-row alignment, before spending the approximately sixteen-minute full-pack materialization pass.

L-155 — Bounded real DTW-store build gate prepared

L-159 — Full-universe DTW store builder prepared

Implementation: The full builder binds the independently verified bounded receipt, the complete immutable packed generation, all 11,584 symbols in physical order, every causal source-prefix identity and the fixed query-independent 788-byte row contract. Only after all shards complete does the builder concatenate them in packed order, verify the content-addressed generation, scan every main and overflow row, check the frozen 90-second/3.1-GiB gates and confirm the resident source lease did not change.

The full build has not started, and no query future or final-period outcome has been opened.

L-172 — Outcome-blind neighbor baseline semantics implemented

Deterministic random: Eligible symbols are ordered by domain-separated SHA-256 of query ID and symbol; within each selected symbol, eligible episodes are ordered by a separate query/episode hash. This produces up to 20 distinct-symbol historical neighbors without using a distance, label or outcome and is invariant to physical input order.

Recent return/volatility: Each 64-session prefix yields 20-session close return, 63-session close return and sample standard deviation of the final 20 log returns. For a query, each feature receives a normalized ordinal rank among all causally eligible episodes plus the query, with value then episode-ID tie-breaking. Exact unweighted L1 rank distance is ordered by distance/episode ID and constrained to one episode per symbol.

Pre-store gates: Synthetic tests verify exact window formulas, missing/invalid refusal, random permutation invariance, distinct-symbol selection and equality of vectorized rank-L1 results with a separately written scalar ordinal oracle including repeated-symbol competition and non-finite exclusion. The contract explicitly records that no outcomes or labels are used.

Next: Build an immutable float64 three-feature generation aligned one-for-one with the packed main/overflow rows. First verify byte alignment, raw reconstruction, rank/permutation equality and three-probe performance on a bounded store; only then publish the full 3,786,156-row feature store.

L-173 — Return/volatility feature-store substrate implemented

Layout: Each packed main and overflow episode receives one aligned 24-byte row containing three little-endian binary64 values. Invalid source windows use three NaNs; partial missingness and infinities fail closed. Binary64 is intentional because rank order—not approximate magnitude—defines the frozen baseline distance.

Identity: The manifest binds the packed generation/provenance, feature and neighbor contracts, exact main/overflow counts, content hashes and outcome-blind provenance. Generation identity is the canonical hash of that state. Loading rechecks manifest identity, packed binding, sizes, optional complete hashes and record invariants.

Tests: Round-trip main/overflow publication, wholly missing rows, changed packed identity, partial missingness and empty-array mapping pass. No full-universe feature generation has been built yet.

Next: Implement a bounded raw-data builder that proves physical symbol-slice alignment, direct feature reconstruction, resumable shard publication and exact rank-neighbor equality before full-universe construction.

L-174 — Vectorized baseline feature extraction fixed

Kernel: Arbitrary packed cutoff positions are evaluated together through 64-session strided windows. The kernel computes exact 20/63-session returns and final-20-log-return sample volatility, marks any nonpositive/non-finite window wholly missing and rejects invalid positions. The scalar API delegates to the same canonical kernel.

Gate: Random positive price paths at boundary, adjacent, interior and final positions are bit-identical to separately sliced scalar NumPy formulas at zero tolerance. Empty positions and short scalar histories behave explicitly.

Next: Use this kernel in the bounded physical-alignment POC, independently reconstruct a deterministic raw sample and compare vector rank neighbors with a shuffled exhaustive oracle before authorizing a full feature generation.

L-182 — Exact baseline retrieval accelerated for monthly scale

Measured baseline: On the late 3,683,053-eligible-row query, the original deterministic-random path took 6.59 seconds and exhaustive rank-L1 took 7.66 seconds. Eight-process execution across 3,936 queries would therefore remain near two hours.

Random optimization: Symbol ordering is independent of episode ordering, so the exact algorithm first hashes all eligible symbols, retains the first 20, then hashes episodes only within those symbols. An independently written exhaustive oracle reproduces exact episode, symbol and order-key outputs. The real late query falls to 0.108 seconds (about 61×).

Rank optimization: Three global feature/episode-ID orders are built once. Each query computes exact causal ordinal ranks through cumulative eligible masks, includes the query's insertion shift and ID tie rule, reduces to the exact minimum (distance, episode ID) per symbol and sorts only symbol winners. Random/tied synthetic data and shuffled traversal exactly match the exhaustive implementation.

Real parity and performance: Building the reusable full index takes 5.51 seconds. The late indexed query takes 0.598 seconds versus 7.124 seconds exhaustive (11.9×), returning the exact same 20 neighbors. No approximation, outcome or label is involved.

L-183 — All-3,936-query baseline batch prepared

Architecture: One parent loads the independently verified packed/feature generations and builds three immutable global rank orders, then eight forked workers reconstruct the 3,936 frozen registry queries. Each worker applies the exact 60-session causal gap and emits 20 distinct deterministic-random plus 20 distinct indexed rank-L1 neighbors into a create-only sealed query receipt.

Durability: Query receipts bind case/query identity and feature generation. Resume accepts only a valid seal with exact upstream identity and neighbor counts. Atomic progress advances every 24 completed queries (one month-equivalent); the root terminal commits ordered case seals/hashes without embedding outcomes.

Real worker POC: The actual first 2012 registry query builds in 0.644 seconds after a 6.50-second full rank index, exposes 1,671,846 eligible episodes, returns 20 neighbors from both methods and reloads the same sealed receipt on resume. Eight focused contract/parity tests pass.

Boundary: This batch includes warmup, development, validation and final query charts but opens no subsequent return, label or setup outcome. The result authorizes only independent retrieval verification, not outcome scoring.

L-184 — V1 baseline batch retains absent-query-symbol refusal

Retained refusal: H0 cbe6b97 and sole-child H1 817cb3a published 245 sealed outcome-blind cases (durable progress through 216 ordered queries) before encountering a registry query symbol absent from the packed historical dictionary. The worker raised instead of applying the packed scanner's established absent-symbol semantics; no root result was published.

Diagnosis: Query eligibility only needs a packed symbol ID to exclude overlapping episodes of that same symbol. If the query symbol is absent from the candidate dictionary, such candidates cannot exist, so the correct packed-scanner behavior is symbol_id=None with no same-symbol exclusion. All global cutoff, episode-ID and quality gates still apply.

Repair: V2 uses an explicit query-symbol lookup returning an ID or None, with a regression test for both present and absent cases and fresh preregistration/output schemas. The partial v1 receipts remain retained and are not reused.

Boundary: No forward outcome, label or final-period result was accessed. V2 must pass its code gates and new H0/H1 freeze before execution.

L-185 — All 3,936 baseline query receipts published

Frozen lineage: Repaired H0 e88d001 and sole-child H1 7e9cfdb bind the verified 90.9-MB feature generation, registry digest, all ordered query IDs, eight-worker execution and absent-symbol semantics. V1's 245 partial receipts remain isolated and unused.

Result: V2 published all 3,936 create-only query receipts across 164 months in 628.0 seconds, including exactly 3,360 scored and 576 warmup queries. Eligible inventory ranges from 657,644 to 3,696,244 historical episodes. The shared rank index built in 6.27 seconds; result digest is becb098e…1d5c.

Boundary: Receipts contain only query metadata, eligibility counts and 20 deterministic-random plus 20 return/volatility neighbors. No subsequent return, setup label, historical outcome or final-period outcome was opened. Independent batch verification is still required before baseline retrieval is marked complete.

L-186 — Independent all-query baseline verifier prepared

Complete structural audit: The verifier revalidates the frozen producer Git blobs, its own clean runtime blob, full feature-generation content and every one of 3,936 case seals. All 157,440 neighbor references must resolve to the immutable packed inventory with matching symbol, cutoff at or before the query's eligibility boundary, distinct-symbol count and valid random order key or finite hexadecimal rank distance.

Overlap audit: Any published neighbor sharing the query symbol triggers raw query reconstruction and must end strictly before the query window begins. This closes the same-symbol causal predicate without loading all query files unnecessarily.

Independent oracle: Four deterministic queries per fold (24 total across warmup, development, three validation folds and final untouched charts) are recomputed with independently written causal masks and random selection plus the exhaustive sort-based rank implementation—not the indexed producer path. Query feature bytes, eligible counts and both complete 20-neighbor lists must match.

Boundary: The verifier reads chart prefixes and retrieval receipts only. It has no outcome or label path and cannot authorize scoring unless every structural and stratified oracle gate passes.

L-187 — All-query baseline retrieval independently verified

Result: Commit dbb0297's independent verifier passed in 63.1 seconds. It verified all 3,936 query receipts and all 157,440 neighbor references, including seals, feature-generation identity, symbol references, global and same-symbol causal cutoffs, deterministic-random order keys and finite hexadecimal rank distances. Verification digest is fd11bf98…4c9b6.

Independent oracle: Twenty-four stratified queries—four from each of warmup, development, three validation folds and final untouched chart prefixes—matched independent random selection and the exhaustive non-indexed return/volatility rank implementation exactly. Forty cases containing same-symbol neighbors were reconstructed from raw prefixes and passed the strict non-overlap predicate.

Boundary: Neither the producer nor verifier opened subsequent returns, setup labels, historical query outcomes or final-period outcomes. This closes the random and return/volatility baseline retrieval lane only; combined price-plus-DTW retrieval for the same 3,936 queries remains required before outcome evaluation can begin.

L-188 — Exact DTW completion moved to a compiled batch kernel

Bottleneck removed: Combined-component completion previously performed channel assembly, pair scaling, allocation and a compiled DTW call separately for every candidate. The new batch boundary packs each symbol group's four 64-sample channels once and completes all candidates in one no-GIL compiled loop while preserving distance-v1 scaling, stable predecessor order and path-length normalization.

Correctness and speed: Focused component and combined-search exhaustive-oracle tests pass, including partial/absent channel coverage. A deterministic 1,040-row benchmark measured 9.832 seconds for scalar completion and 0.042 seconds for the batch kernel (232.2×), with maximum absolute deviation 2.22e-16 and no ranking change. The complete repository test suite also passes.

Boundary: This changes only exact distance computation after a candidate is admitted. Proposal generation and strict next-bound closure are unchanged, and no outcome or label was read.

L-189 — Price-component packed bound no longer computes unrelated channels

Optimization: Price-only proposal and threshold scans now evaluate exactly the six distance-v1 price channels. They no longer unpack or compute coarse, stage, structural, candle/volatility, volume/shock or market-context bounds that cannot affect price-component ranking.

Parity and speed: The specialized branch-aware result is bit-for-bit equal to the full kernel's price component in synthetic family coverage and a real 65,536-row packed slice. That real slice improved from 1.521 to 0.395 seconds (3.85×). Focused search tests and the complete repository suite pass.

Boundary: Composite retrieval still uses every component. This specialization changes neither eligibility nor the price lower-bound formula and opens no outcome or label.

L-190 — Exact staged price-plus-DTW search closes the real authority 6.67× faster

Certified cascade: The staged search exactly completes a stable 16,384-row rigid-price-bound seed to establish a finite top-20 upper bound, retains every eligible rigid bound equal to or below it, evaluates the stronger DTW bound only for those rows, and exactly completes every combined bound equal to or below it. Only strict-greater-than rows are pruned, preserving ties and distinct-symbol selection.

Specialized reconstruction: Component completion now materializes only the six 48-sample rigid-price channels and four 64-sample DTW channels it consumes. Unit coverage proves these projections, rigid distances and completed price distances are bit-identical to full distance-v1 representations.

Real authority parity: On the frozen early case, all 20 episode IDs, ordering and hexadecimal distances exactly match the prior scalar combined result. The cascade classified 1,961,577 eligible rows, admitted 638,971 past rigid price, admitted 72,531 past combined DTW and exactly completed 72,646 rows including the seed. End-to-end time fell from 941.3 to 141.1 seconds (6.67× overall); against the first staged implementation it fell from 218.4 to 141.1 seconds. The complete repository suite passes.

Profile: Rigid and DTW bound phases now take 8.0 and 12.1 seconds. Seed and final exact reconstruction take 63.6 and 56.4 seconds, identifying cross-query source reuse as the next optimization target.

Boundary: The run used only historical chart prefixes and independently verified packed/DTW stores. No subsequent return, outcome, label or final-period result was opened.

L-191 — Explicit inventory cache prepared for multi-query retrieval

Design: Batch mode can now wrap any OHLCV source in a thread-safe LRU or inventory-sized cache, preload distinct instruments concurrently and borrow cached canonical frames through an explicit read-only interface. Default directory-source behavior remains the existing 32-symbol cache.

Observed tradeoff: Preloading all 12,080 NASDAQ frames took 73.0 seconds. A subsequent frozen early-case retrieval remained hexadecimal-exact and took 121.6 seconds versus 141.1 seconds without the inventory cache, at 12.6 GiB observed peak RSS on the currently exposed 31-GiB/8-CPU environment. The cache recorded 12,080 misses and 2,374 retrieval hits.

Further reduction: Exact price projections now share one empty immutable channel placeholder instead of constructing one pandas frame per candidate and borrow cached frames without defensive copies. A 1,000-window specialized projection takes 0.359 seconds. Focused cache/isolation tests and the complete repository suite pass.

Boundary: The cache is an execution optimization over immutable historical chart prefixes. It does not cache or expose outcomes, labels or future rows.

L-192 — Same-month combined-retrieval concurrency POC prepared

Scope: Four frozen March 2014 development queries spanning four registry liquidity cells will run concurrently against one preloaded canonical source cache. Each query receives two of the eight exposed CPUs for rigid bounds, DTW bounds and exact completion; output order remains registry order.

Acceptance: All four staged certificates must complete with zero process swap and no outcome/label access. The included OHAI authority must reproduce all 20 frozen scalar episode IDs, symbols, cutoffs and hexadecimal distances exactly. Timing, cache state and peak RSS are observational and excluded from semantic case digests.

Decision boundary: This POC selects the safe concurrency policy for the durable 3,936-query producer. It cannot authorize outcome evaluation or production promotion.

L-193 — Four-query outer concurrency rejected as the production default

Frozen execution: The preregistered March 2014 POC completed four simultaneous queries with two inner threads each against one preloaded 12,080-symbol cache. All four certificates closed, the frozen OHAI authority reproduced all 20 episode IDs and hexadecimal distances exactly, process swap remained zero and neither outcomes nor labels were opened.

Measured result: Inventory preload took 74.8 seconds and the concurrent query phase took 408.8 seconds. Individual queries took 150.3–408.4 seconds and observed peak RSS reached 38,402.9 MiB. The immutable result digest is fb2628d6…b479.

Decision: Four outer queries competing for two threads each provide insufficient throughput benefit for their memory and per-query latency cost. This tested configuration is retained as evidence but rejected as the durable producer default; sequential queries may instead reuse prepared data while each query uses all exposed compute.

L-194 — Exact symbol preparation is reusable across sequential queries

Optimization: Exact completion now converts each immutable symbol to timestamp, OHLCV and benchmark-aligned NumPy arrays once and accepts an explicit batch-lifetime prepared-symbol cache. Later queries reuse those arrays while independently reconstructing their requested windows. Cached entries retain and recheck both stock and benchmark causal-prefix bindings; the generic source interface remains supported.

Exactness and speed: Two consecutive frozen OHAI searches produced the same authority-exact result digest 7af4efe6…7f46. The first took 127.8 seconds and the reused query took 69.8 seconds. A warm exact seed ladder kept all 20 results hexadecimal-exact; 2,048 seed rows was fastest at 62.7 seconds versus 62.9 for 512, 66.6 for 8,192 and 70.4 for 16,384.

Verification: A repeated-query regression proves equal matches and no candidate-symbol source reloads after preparation. Focused cache, exact-batch and combined-search tests pass 29/29; the complete repository suite passes 989/989 and git diff --check is clean.

L-195 — Progressive exact completion tested and rejected

Hypothesis: Process combined-bound candidates in increasing batches, recompute the distinct-symbol threshold after each batch and skip later candidates above the tighter threshold.

Measured rejection: The frozen OHAI result remained hexadecimal-exact, but elapsed time regressed to 291.5 seconds. Only 529 same-symbol rows and 1,318 globally bounded rows were avoided, while repeated per-symbol projection across batches dominated execution. The experiment therefore failed its performance purpose.

Disposition: The progressive implementation was fully removed. Exact completion remains one grouped pass over every candidate at or below the certified seed threshold. This negative result is retained here to prevent an unsupported retry.

L-196 — Durable all-query combined retrieval producer prepared

Execution contract: The 3,936 frozen registry queries run sequentially, each using all eight exposed CPUs, one inventory-sized immutable source cache and one batch-lifetime prepared numeric-array cache. The exact seed is frozen at 2,048 rows from the authority-exact timing ladder. Each completed query is published as a create-only sealed receipt and progress is atomically replaced after every query, so interruption can lose only the active query.

Correctness boundaries: Resume independently validates each case seal, query/fold/upstream identities, certificate count relations, strict rigid and combined bound closure, exact distinct-symbol ordering, final threshold, and a reconstructed staged-search result digest. Packed resident and DTW file identities are rechecked after every newly computed case; any identity drift or process swap refuses publication. Runtime dependencies include no outcome or label module.

Pre-scale evidence: Ten dedicated contract/refusal tests plus the staged-search tests pass. The receipt validator also accepts the real frozen OHAI certificate and independently reconstructs authority result digest 7af4efe6…7f46. The complete repository suite passes 999/999 and the HTML/diff checks are clean. H0/H1 freezing remains required before launch.

L-197 — Independent all-query combined verifier prepared

Complete audit: The verifier independently validates every producer/case seal, reconstructs all staged certificate result digests, normalizes the physically distinct main and overflow store layouts, resolves all 78,720 analogue episode IDs, and checks symbol, cutoff, quality tier, causal gap and strict same-symbol non-overlap against the immutable packed inventory. Aggregate case-file hashes, semantic digests and eligible-count extrema must reproduce the terminal result.

Independent inputs and reruns: Full packed and DTW generation content is rehashed and record-validated. The previously frozen scalar OHAI authority must match exactly. One deterministic query from each of the six folds is then reconstructed and rerun with the preregistered algorithm, requiring exact matches and certificate result digests. No outcome or label path is accepted.

Pre-run verification: Five focused verifier tests cover deterministic fold sampling, independent episode lookup, physical-layout normalization, seal mutation and certificate sensitivity; all focused producer/verifier tests pass 15/15. The complete repository suite passes 1,004/1,004 under concurrent producer load. The verifier can execute only after all 3,936 producer receipts are terminal.
